## Enumeration and confidence

The current `catalog.csv` and the 40 module catalogs each enumerate **733 distinct exact actions**. Each action is kept separate, including create/read/update/delete, state transitions, source references, support paths and local utilities. These are action-level inventory items, not 733 independently accepted product features. Catalog design labels are preserved separately from current whole-module IN_PROGRESS declarations. A design label “Resolved delegated” does not mean implemented. A literal action string in a policy, service, screen or test does not prove a registered, reachable or correct vertical flow. Exact-token absence does not prove absence when code composes actions dynamically.

Search scope: first-party `.cs` under `src`/`tests` (excluding bin/obj), `.ts`/`.tsx` under `web/Nexora.Web/src`, current Program.cs endpoint registration, current feature/API directories and existing implementation reports. `rg` and `python` were unavailable on this agent PATH; PowerShell file enumeration and regex were used. All code candidates below were re-indexed from the current checkout, rather than copied from the older lexical CSV. The index retains at most 80 references per exact token and displays selected candidates, so an absent frontend/test candidate is not proof that no such code/test exists. Review depth is enumeration/candidate reconciliation, not a full semantic review of every handler/SQL transaction/React state.

Known absent vertical families are corroborated by the actual registered endpoint families, current API/Application directory inventory and the latest explicit backlog in reports 12/14/08. They remain committed R1 backlog unless a PO decision explicitly changes scope. They are **not automatically deferred to the next release**, and advanced gates are **not blanket scope exclusions**. Retired Superseded actions remain catalogued but are excluded from active implementation acceptance.

| Inventory metric | Result / limit |
|---|---|
| Catalog actions / FX scopes | 733 / 40 |
| Current module catalog actions | 733; exact ID equality checked below |
| Catalog CSV explicitly SLICE_IMPLEMENTED | 45; snapshot declaration only, later increments are additional |
| Runtime tests executed by this workstream | 0 cases / 0 runs; all per-action execution cells NOT TESTED |
| Verified-complete / Release-ready actions from this inventory | 0 / 0; no acceptance inference from source references |
| Overall inventory release recommendation | HOLD: ordinary committed implementation gaps and mandatory acceptance/review evidence remain |

No PASS cell is assigned by this inventory workstream. In initial snapshot rows, Backend/frontend PARTIAL means candidate source was located, not test execution. In24 report19 delta rows, PARTIAL explicitly combines bounded implementation, independently reviewed slices and executed selected tests with still-incomplete full acceptance. Order/Warranty browser CRUD remains NOT TESTED in report19; generic E2E PARTIAL denotes only the scanner/other family observations and must not be read as CRUD proof. A test source reference alone denotes a candidate test, not a passing run. Suite counts do not fill whole-action acceptance cells.

## Screen, form and permission-path checklist

The complete per-action UI binding is retained in the full inventory rows. For **each** referenced screen and each applicable action/context, the acceptance checklist is: authorized navigation/list/detail/create/edit/confirmation; current permission loss; unavailable/disabled source; loading/empty/error/success; server validation and boundary fields; stale ETag/conflict; duplicate submission/uncertain-write/retry; dirty draft/cancel/back/return path; desktop/mobile layout; keyboard/focus and accessible names; owner/source isolation; persisted refresh. Each checklist item is **NOT TESTED by this workstream**. Screens are planned UI contracts, not proof that a runtime screen exists. Existing `popup-disposition.csv` is the detailed dialog candidate register, not accepted UX coverage.

## Discrepancies and remaining audit work

- Current module catalogs and `catalog.csv` are design snapshots; the effective Sept9 overlay still says SavedSearch/Recents/Command Palette and Collections are gated/missing, while current Program.cs registers those endpoint families and Oct24 reports document local code. Preserve both statuses; use latest implementation evidence for presence and current decisions for permission, not old snapshot prose.
- Historical ledger baseline says Windows ACL is incomplete/failed; latest PR description reports a later successful genuine-principal CI job. This workstream has not retrieved those artifacts and does not turn the old claim or newer summary into a fresh per-feature PASS.
- D01 Vault remains docs-only regardless of generic DEC-014 local authority. D02/D03 are bounded implementation gates, not approved removal from R1. FX30/34/35 remain real-execution paused and are not moved to R2.
- The earlier lexical CSV source revision differs from this checkout. This report re-indexes current code but still does not complete its semantic AC mapping. Exact-token hits in policy lists can coexist with missing handlers; dynamic action composition can coexist with no literal hit.
- Full reverse endpoint/service/SQL/UI mapping, every current source requirement beyond the 733 action contracts, every screen/dialog state, unreachable paths, per-operation test assertions and independent release signoff remain open. Enumeration covers all catalog actions, not a claim that 100% of all possible user-facing behavior has been audited.

- Module table rows: 733; missing catalog IDs: 0; extra module IDs: 0.

- Known absent family rows (including policy metadata where present): 121; source-reference PARTIAL rows: 372; remaining UNKNOWN rows: 229. These are inventory classifications, not acceptance counts.
- No unit, SQL/API, browser, functional, regression, performance or accessibility execution performed by inventory agent. No product files, test files, fixtures or configuration changed.

Programmatic report validation (PowerShell, 2026-10-25): regex-select full inventory rows with `(?m)^\| `([^`]+)` \| FX-\d{2} \|`, quality rows by their `UNKNOWN / NEEDS VERIFICATION` prefix and screen rows with `(?m)^\| (FX\d{2}-S\d{2}) \|`; assert row counts equal unique-ID counts; `Compare-Object` full IDs with catalog.ActionKey and quality IDs with non-Superseded catalog keys. Actual result: **PASS inventory733/unique733; quality722/unique722; screens201/unique201; both ID sets equal**. This validates document enumeration consistency only, not application behavior.
